function loadPack(id){if(packs[id])return Promise.resolve(packs[id]);if(packLoading[id])return packLoading[id];return packLoading[id]=Promise.all(['a','b'].map(h=>fetch('pack-'+id+'-'+h+'.txt?'+(location.search||'?1').slice(1)).then(x=>{if(!x.ok)throw 0;return x.json()}))).then(js=>{let j=Object.assign({},...js);packs[id]=j;return j}).catch(()=>{packLoading[id]=null;return null})}
function museumStamps(m){return (state.rooms||[]).filter(x=>x.startsWith(m.id+'-')).length}

function roomView(){let r=museum.rooms[roomIndex],n=museum.rooms.length,key=museum.id+'-'+roomIndex,got=(state.rooms||[]).includes(key),opts=shuffle([r.a,...r.wrong]);roomSolved=false;

let CC=null;
function ccYear(d){const m=String(d||'').match(/(\d{4})/);return m?+m[1]:0}
function ccCent(x){return Math.floor(ccYear(x.date)/100)*100}
function ccBriefs(){
  const pool=poolAll().filter(x=>x.img&&x.artist&&ccYear(x.date));
  const byA={};pool.forEach(x=>{(byA[x.artist]=byA[x.artist]||[]).push(x)});
  const briefs=[];
  shuffle(Object.keys(byA).filter(a=>byA[a].length>=3)).slice(0,3).forEach(a=>{
    const match=shuffle(byA[a].slice()).slice(0,3);
    const cent=ccCent(match[0]);
    let dis=shuffle(pool.filter(x=>x.artist!==a&&ccCent(x)===cent)).slice(0,6);
    if(dis.length<6)dis=dis.concat(shuffle(pool.filter(x=>x.artist!==a&&ccCent(x)!==cent)).slice(0,6-dis.length));
    if(dis.length===6)briefs.push({t:'Hang only works by '+a,match,dis});
  });
  shuffle([1500,1600,1700,1800,1900]).slice(0,2).forEach(c=>{
    const inC=pool.filter(x=>ccCent(x)===c),outC=pool.filter(x=>ccCent(x)!==c);
    if(inC.length>=3&&outC.length>=6)briefs.push({t:'Hang only works from the '+(c/100|0)+'00s',match:shuffle(inC).slice(0,3),dis:shuffle(outC).slice(0,6)});
  });
  return shuffle(briefs);
}
function ccSwap(i){
  const b=CC.briefs[CC.i],t=CC.tiles[i];
  const pool=poolAll().filter(x=>x.img&&x.artist&&ccYear(x.date));
  const used={};CC.tiles.forEach(u=>{used[u.w.id]=1});
  let cand;
  if(t.m){
    if(b.a)cand=shuffle((pool.filter(x=>x.artist===b.a&&!used[x.id])))[0];
  }else{
    cand=shuffle(pool.filter(x=>(!b.a||x.artist!==b.a)&&(!b.c||ccCent(x)!==b.c)&&!used[x.id]))[0];
  }
  if(!cand)return;
  CC.tiles[i]={w:cand,m:t.m};
  const el=document.querySelector('.cc-tile[data-i="'+i+'"]');
  if(el){const im=el.querySelector('img');im.src=cand.img;el.querySelector('span').textContent=cand.title;delete CC.sel[i];el.classList.remove('sel')}
}
function ccBrief(){
  const b=CC.briefs[CC.i];
  CC.sel={};CC.t0=Date.now();CC.done=false;
  CC.tiles=shuffle(b.match.map(x=>({w:x,m:1,a:b.a,c:b.c})).concat(b.dis.map(x=>({w:x,m:0,a:b.a,c:b.c}))));
  shell('<button class="ghost" data-route="play">← Games</button><div class="eyebrow">Curator\'s Choice · '+(CC.i+1)+' / '+CC.briefs.length+' · Score '+CC.score+'</div><h1>'+E(b.t)+'</h1><p class="muted">Tap the three works that belong on this wall, then hang it.</p><div class="cc-grid">'+CC.tiles.map((t,i)=>'<button type="button" class="cc-tile" data-i="'+i+'" aria-pressed="false"><img src="'+E(t.w.img)+'" alt="'+E(t.w.title)+'" referrerpolicy="no-referrer" loading="lazy" decoding="async"><span>'+E(t.w.title)+'</span></button>').join('')+'</div><button class="primary" id="ccHang">Hang the wall</button>');
  document.querySelectorAll('.cc-tile').forEach(el=>{
    el.onclick=()=>{if(CC.done)return;const i=+el.dataset.i;CC.sel[i]=!CC.sel[i];el.classList.toggle('sel',!!CC.sel[i]);el.setAttribute('aria-pressed',CC.sel[i]?'true':'false')};
    el.querySelector('img').onerror=()=>ccSwap(+el.dataset.i);
  });
  $('#ccHang').onclick=ccJudge;
}
function ccJudge(){
  if(CC.done)return;CC.done=true;
  let good=0,bad=0;
  CC.tiles.forEach((t,i)=>{if(CC.sel[i]){if(t.m)good++;else bad++}});
  let pts=good*10-bad*5;
  const perfect=good===3&&bad===0;
  if(perfect){pts+=5;CC.perfect++}
  CC.score+=pts;
  document.querySelectorAll('.cc-tile').forEach((el,i)=>{
    const t=CC.tiles[i];
    el.classList.remove('sel');
    el.classList.add(t.m?'cc-right':(CC.sel[i]?'cc-wrong':'cc-missed'));
    el.querySelector('span').innerHTML=E(t.w.title)+'<br><span class="cc-cap">'+E(t.w.artist)+' · '+E(t.w.date)+'</span>';
  });
  const b=CC.briefs[CC.i];
  const line=perfect?'Perfect wall! +'+pts+' points':(good+' right, '+bad+' wrong · '+(pts>=0?'+':'')+pts+' points');
  const last=CC.i+1>=CC.briefs.length;
  $('#ccHang').outerHTML='<p><b>'+line+'</b></p><button class="primary" id="ccNext">'+(last?'See the gallery':'Next wall')+'</button>';
  $('#ccNext').onclick=()=>{CC.i++;if(last)ccEnd();else ccBrief()};
}
function ccEnd(){
  if(CC.score>0&&!CC.rec){CC.rec=1;try{PAS.add('curator|classic',"Curator's Choice, points",CC.score,'pts',false,CC.perfect+' of '+CC.briefs.length+' perfect walls')}catch(e){}}
  state.stars+=CC.perfect;save();
  shell('<div class="card"><h1>The wall is hung</h1><h2>'+CC.score+' points</h2><p>'+CC.perfect+' of '+CC.briefs.length+' perfect walls. Every piece is a real public-domain artwork.</p><button class="primary" id="ccAgain">Curate again</button><button data-route="play">More games</button></div>');
  $('#ccAgain').onclick=curatorGame;
}
window.curatorGame=function(){
  CC={briefs:ccBriefs(),i:0,score:0,perfect:0,rec:0};
  if(CC.briefs.length<3){shell('<div class="card"><h1>Curator\'s Choice</h1><p>Artworks are still loading. Try again in a moment.</p><button data-route="play">← Games</button></div>');return}
  ccBrief();
};
})();

function sh(c,f){return c.map(function(v){return Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(f<=1?v*f:v+(255-v)*(f-1))))})}

/* Crumble Climb rules (original). Cells: 0 air, 1 brick, 2 stone, 3 spikes (deadly to land on), 4 cracked brick (crumbles after you step off). Actions: L R move/climb, S smash facing, U smash above, D smash underfoot. */
/* Crumble Climb engine v4. Tiles: 0 air, 1 brick (smashable), 2 stone (solid), 3 spikes (solid, deadly to land on),
   4 cracked brick (smashable, crumbles when stepped off), 5/6 warp portal pair A and 7/8 pair B (solid like stone;
   entering one horizontally teleports the hero to its same-pair cell; inspired by verified Popils warp doors),
   9 beast (solid, deadly to land on - it eats you).
   Actions: L R move/climb, S smash facing block at hero row, U smash block above, D smash block underfoot. */
var POP=(function(){
function at(s,x,y){if(x<0||x>=s.w||y<0||y>=s.h)return 2;return s.g[y*s.w+x]}
function clone(s){return{w:s.w,h:s.h,g:s.g.slice(),x:s.x,y:s.y,d:s.d,ex:s.ex,ey:s.ey,dead:false}}
function fall(s){while(at(s,s.x,s.y+1)===0)s.y++}
function settle(s,x){for(var y=s.h-2;y>=0;y--){var v=s.g[y*s.w+x];if(v===1||v===4){var ny=y;while(at(s,x,ny+1)===0&&!(s.x===x&&s.y===ny+1))ny++;if(ny!==y){s.g[y*s.w+x]=0;s.g[ny*s.w+x]=v}}}}
function smashable(v){return v===1||v===4}
function portal(v){return v>=5&&v<=8}
function pairOf(s,v){var other=v%2===1?v+1:v-1;for(var i=0;i<s.g.length;i++)if(s.g[i]===other)return{x:i%s.w,y:Math.floor(i/s.w)};return null}
function hazard(s){var b=at(s,s.x,s.y+1);if(b===3){s.dead=true;s.deadBy="spikes"}else if(b===9){s.dead=true;s.deadBy="animal"}}
function crumbleFrom(s,ox,oy){ /* hero left (ox,oy): cracked support below old cell crumbles */
 if(oy+1<s.h&&s.g[(oy+1)*s.w+ox]===4){s.g[(oy+1)*s.w+ox]=0;settle(s,ox)}}
function apply(s,a){var n=clone(s),d;
 if(a==="L"||a==="R"){d=a==="L"?-1:1;var tx=s.x+d,t=at(s,tx,s.y),ox,oy;
  if(t===0){ox=s.x;oy=s.y;n.x=tx;n.d=d;fall(n);crumbleFrom(n,ox,oy);hazard(n);return n}
  if(portal(t)){var pr=pairOf(s,t);
   if(!pr)return null; /* unpaired portal: behaves solid */
   ox=s.x;oy=s.y;n.x=pr.x;n.y=pr.y;n.d=d;fall(n);crumbleFrom(n,ox,oy);hazard(n);return n}
  if(at(s,s.x,s.y-1)===0&&at(s,tx,s.y-1)===0){ox=s.x;oy=s.y;n.x=tx;n.y=s.y-1;n.d=d;crumbleFrom(n,ox,oy);hazard(n);return n}
  if(s.d!==d){n.d=d;return n}return null}
 if(a==="S"){var bx=s.x+s.d;if(!smashable(at(s,bx,s.y)))return null;n.g[s.y*s.w+bx]=0;settle(n,bx);return n}
 if(a==="U"){if(!smashable(at(s,s.x,s.y-1)))return null;n.g[(s.y-1)*s.w+s.x]=0;settle(n,s.x);return n}
 if(a==="D"){if(!smashable(at(s,s.x,s.y+1)))return null;n.g[(s.y+1)*s.w+s.x]=0;fall(n);settle(n,s.x);hazard(n);return n}
 return null}
function won(s){return!s.dead&&s.x===s.ex&&s.y===s.ey}
function key(s){return s.g.join("")+","+s.x+","+s.y+","+s.d}
var ACTS=["L","R","S","U","D"];
function solve(s0,cap,acts){cap=cap||250000;acts=acts||ACTS;var seen={},nodes=[s0],from=[-1],act=[""];seen[key(s0)]=1;var head=0;
 while(head<nodes.length){var s=nodes[head];if(won(s)){var p=[],k=head;while(from[k]>=0){p.push(act[k]);k=from[k]}p.reverse();return{par:p.length,smashes:p.filter(function(c){return c==="S"||c==="U"||c==="D"}).length,path:p}}
  if(nodes.length>cap)return null;
  if(!s.dead)for(var i=0;i<acts.length;i++){var n=apply(s,acts[i]);if(!n)continue;var kk=key(n);if(seen[kk])continue;seen[kk]=1;nodes.push(n);from.push(head);act.push(acts[i])}
  head++}
 return null}
function usedPortal(s0,path){var s=s0;for(var i=0;i<path.length;i++){var n=apply(s,path[i]);if(!n)return false;if(Math.abs(n.x-s.x)>1||Math.abs(n.y-s.y)>1)return true;s=n}return false}
if(typeof module!=="undefined")module.exports={at:at,apply:apply,won:won,solve:solve,clone:clone,settle:settle,pairOf:pairOf,usedPortal:usedPortal};

 else if(k===3){c.fillStyle="#d8f6ff";var gg=c.createLinearGradient(x,y+8*u,x+s,y+32*u);gg.addColorStop(0,"#bff4ff");gg.addColorStop(1,"#4fb6e8");c.fillStyle=gg;rr(c,x+8*u,y+7*u,24*u,24*u,3*u);c.fill();c.strokeStyle="#4a2fb0";c.lineWidth=2*u;c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+20*u,y+7*u);c.lineTo(x+20*u,y+31*u);c.moveTo(x+8*u,y+19*u);c.lineTo(x+32*u,y+19*u);c.stroke();c.fillStyle="#d7c9ff";c.fillRect(x+5*u,y+31*u,30*u,4*u);c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.7)";c.fillRect(x+11*u,y+10*u,5*u,3*u)}
 else if(k===4){c.fillStyle="#145c45";c.fillRect(x+6*u,y+27*u,28*u,6*u);var dg=c.createRadialGradient(x+16*u,y+16*u,2*u,x+20*u,y+27*u,16*u);dg.addColorStop(0,"#d9ffee");dg.addColorStop(1,"#35b88c");c.fillStyle=dg;c.beginPath();c.arc(x+20*u,y+27*u,13*u,Math.PI,0);c.closePath();c.fill();c.fillStyle="#145c45";c.fillRect(x+19*u,y+7*u,2*u,6*u);c.beginPath();c.arc(x+20*u,y+6*u,2.2*u,0,7);c.fill();c.strokeStyle="rgba(20,92,69,.5)";c.lineWidth=1.2*u;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+20*u,y+14*u);c.lineTo(x+20*u,y+27*u);c.moveTo(x+13*u,y+17*u);c.lineTo(x+13*u,y+27*u);c.moveTo(x+27*u,y+17*u);c.lineTo(x+27*u,y+27*u);c.stroke()}
 else{c.fillStyle="#7a5208";c.fillRect(x+5*u,y+7*u,30*u,6*u);c.fillRect(x+5*u,y+27*u,30*u,6*u);c.fillRect(x+17*u,y+12*u,6*u,16*u);c.fillStyle="#fff1b8";c.fillRect(x+5*u,y+7*u,30*u,2*u);c.fillRect(x+5*u,y+27*u,30*u,2*u);c.fillRect(x+17*u,y+12*u,2*u,16*u);c.fillStyle="#7a5208";for(var j=0;j<3;j++){c.beginPath();c.arc(x+(10+j*10)*u,y+10*u,1.4*u,0,7);c.fill();c.beginPath();c.arc(x+(10+j*10)*u,y+30*u,1.4*u,0,7);c.fill()}}
 c.restore();c.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.55)";c.lineWidth=1.4*(s/40);rr(c,x+m+.7,y+m+.7,s-2*m-1.4,s-2*m-1.4,s*0.17);c.stroke();c.restore()}
function mkSky(){var c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=CW*C;c.height=CH*C;var x=c.getContext("2d"),r=PA.rnd(5);
 var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,CH*C);g.addColorStop(0,"#16224a");g.addColorStop(1,"#0a1026");x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);

 x.fillStyle="rgba(183,162,255,.5)";for(i=0;i<26;i++)x.fillRect(r()*c.width,r()*c.height*0.6,2,2);return c}
function menu(){stop();PA.backs=[];PA.stats("");PA.menu("Arch Columns","Stacks of three building pieces fall down. Use Swap to cycle them, drag on the board to slide them sideways, tap the board to drop fast. Line up three or more of the same kind across, down or diagonally to clear them. Chains score more.",[["Start building",function(){start()}]])}
function stop(){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;S=null}
function start(){stop();PA.$("stage").innerHTML='<style>#agc{max-height:calc(100dvh - 450px)!important;width:min(100%,calc((100dvh - 450px)*0.7))!important;margin:0 auto}</style><canvas id="agc" class="g" width="'+W+'" height="'+H+'"></canvas><div class="ag4pad" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="primary" id="bt" style="flex:0 0 auto;min-width:140px">Swap</button></div><p class="muted" style="text-align:center;margin:8px 0 0">Drag on the board to slide, tap the board to drop fast. Swap cycles the pieces.</p><div id="res"></div>';

 S={g:[],off:[],score:0,gems:0,level:1,piece:null,next:null,t:0,fallT:0,clear:null,chain:0,over:false,soft:false,sq:[]};for(var i=0;i<CW*CH;i++){S.g.push(-1);S.off.push(0)}
 S.next=rndp();spawn();PA.backs=[function(){menu();return true}];bind();stats();last=performance.now();raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
function nTypes(){return Math.min(6,4+Math.floor((S?S.level:1)-1)/2|0)}
function r(){return Math.floor(Math.random()*nTypes())}function rndp(){return[r(),r(),r()]}
function spawn(){S.piece={c:2,y:0,k:S.next};S.next=rndp();S.fallT=0;if(!fits(S.piece.c,0)){S.over=true;end()}}
function g(x,y){return y<0?-1:S.g[y*CW+x]}
function fits(c,y){return y+2<CH&&g(c,y)<0&&g(c,y+1)<0&&g(c,y+2)<0}
function interval(){return Math.max(0.09,0.62-(S.level-1)*0.055)}
function stats(){PA.stats("Score "+S.score+"  Level "+S.level)}
function move(d){if(!S||S.over||S.clear)return;var nc=S.piece.c+d;if(nc<0||nc>=CW)return;if(g(nc,S.piece.y)<0&&g(nc,S.piece.y+1)<0&&g(nc,S.piece.y+2)<0&&(S.piece.y+3>=CH||true)){S.piece.c=nc;PA.beep(330,.03)}}
function rot(){if(!S||S.over||S.clear)return;var k=S.piece.k;S.piece.k=[k[2],k[0],k[1]];PA.beep(520,.04)}
function bind(){PA.$("bt").onclick=rot;
 var sx=null,sy=0,st=0,moved=false;cv.addEventListener("pointerdown",function(e){sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;st=performance.now();moved=false;try{cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(x){}e.preventDefault()});
 cv.addEventListener("pointermove",function(e){if(sx==null)return;var rc=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),cw=rc.width*(C/W)*0.9,dx=e.clientX-sx;if(Math.abs(dx)>cw){move(dx>0?1:-1);sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;moved=true}else if(e.clientY-sy>rc.height/6){if(S)S.soft=true;moved=true}});
 var up=function(){if(sx==null)return;if(!moved&&performance.now()-st<350&&S&&!S.over&&!S.clear)S.fastDrop=true;sx=null;if(S)S.soft=false};cv.addEventListener("pointerup",up);cv.addEventListener("pointercancel",function(){sx=null;if(S)S.soft=false})}
window.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){if(!S||!document.getElementById("agc"))return;if(e.key==="ArrowLeft")move(-1);else if(e.key==="ArrowRight")move(1);else if(e.key==="ArrowUp"||e.key===" "){rot();e.preventDefault()}else if(e.key==="ArrowDown"){S.soft=true;e.preventDefault()}});
window.addEventListener("keyup",function(e){if(S&&e.key==="ArrowDown")S.soft=false});
function loop(t){raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);if(!document.getElementById("agc")){stop();return}var dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(0.05,(t-last)/1000));last=t;if(S)step(dt);draw()}
function canDown(){return S.piece.y+3<CH&&g(S.piece.c,S.piece.y+3)<0}
function step(dt){fx.update(dt);S.t+=dt;for(var i=0;i<S.off.length;i++)if(S.off[i]>0)S.off[i]=Math.max(0,S.off[i]-dt*16);S.sq=S.sq.filter(function(q){q.t-=dt;return q.t>0});
 if(S.over)return;if(S.clear){S.clear.t-=dt;if(S.clear.t<=0)finishClear();return}
 S.fallT+=dt*((S.soft||S.fastDrop)?12:1);if(S.fallT<interval())return;S.fallT=0;if(canDown()){S.piece.y++;return}lock()}
function lock(){S.fastDrop=false;var p=S.piece;for(var i=0;i<3;i++){S.g[(p.y+i)*CW+p.c]=p.k[i];S.sq.push({i:(p.y+i)*CW+p.c,t:.22})}fx.burst((p.c+.5)*C,(p.y+3)*C,6,["#eef4ff","#74d7ff"],{sp:50,life:.35,size:3,g:60});fx.shake(2.5);S.piece=null;PA.beep(170,.06);S.chain=0;resolve()}
function find(){var m={},any=false,D=[[1,0],[0,1],[1,1],[1,-1]];for(var y=0;y<CH;y++)for(var x=0;x<CW;x++){var v=g(x,y);if(v<0)continue;D.forEach(function(d){var px=x-d[0],py=y-d[1];if(px>=0&&px<CW&&py>=0&&py<CH&&g(px,py)===v)return;var n=0,cx=x,cy=y;while(cx>=0&&cx<CW&&cy>=0&&cy<CH&&g(cx,cy)===v){n++;cx+=d[0];cy+=d[1]}if(n>=3){any=true;for(var k=0;k<n;k++)m[(y+d[1]*k)*CW+(x+d[0]*k)]=1}})}return any?m:null}
function resolve(){var m=find();if(!m){S.clear=null;if(!S.over)spawn();return}S.chain++;var n=Object.keys(m).length,cnt={};for(var k in m)cnt[S.g[k]]=(cnt[S.g[k]]||0)+1;var best=0;for(var t in cnt)if(cnt[t]>(cnt[best]||0))best=+t;S.clear={m:m,t:0.38,n:n,best:best};PA.beep(520+S.chain*130,.16,"triangle")}
function finishClear(){var c=S.clear,n=c.n,sx=0,sy=0;for(var k in c.m){var kx=k%CW,ky=Math.floor(k/CW);sx+=kx;sy+=ky;fx.burst((kx+.5)*C,(ky+.5)*C,7,["#fff1b8","#80edbe","#74d7ff","#b7a2ff"],{sp:90,life:.7,up:40,size:4});S.g[k]=-1}
 fx.text((sx/n+.5)*C,(sy/n+.5)*C,NAMES[c.best]+(S.chain>1?" x"+S.chain:"")+" +"+n*10*S.chain*S.level,S.chain>1?"#80edbe":"#ffe08a");fx.shake(3+S.chain*2);
 S.gems+=n;S.score+=n*10*S.chain*S.level;var nl=Math.floor(S.gems/25)+1;if(nl>S.level){S.level=nl;fx.text(CW*C/2,CH*C/2,"Level "+nl,"#74d7ff")}
 for(var x=0;x<CW;x++){var w=CH-1;for(var y=CH-1;y>=0;y--){var v=S.g[y*CW+x];if(v>=0){S.g[y*CW+x]=-1;S.g[w*CW+x]=v;S.off[w*CW+x]=w-y;w--}}}
 S.clear=null;stats();resolve()}
function end(){fx.shake(8);PA.board("archcols","Arch Columns, points",S.score,"pts",false,"level "+S.level);PA.$("res").innerHTML='<div class="ag4msg"><h2>Site full</h2><p>'+S.score+' points, level '+S.level+'</p></div><div class="ag4row"><button class="primary" id="ag">Build again</button><button id="mn">Menu</button></div>';PA.$("ag").onclick=start;PA.$("mn").onclick=menu}
function ghostRow(){var y=S.piece.y;while(y+3<CH&&g(S.piece.c,y+3)<0)y++;return y}
function draw(){if(!S||!cv)return;var c=ctx,o=fx.off();c.save();c.clearRect(0,0,W,H);c.fillStyle="#0a1026";c.fillRect(0,0,W,H);c.translate(o[0],o[1]);c.drawImage(sky,0,0);
 for(var y=0;y<CH;y++)for(var x=0;x<CW;x++){var v=S.g[y*CW+x];if(v<0)continue;var fl=S.clear&&S.clear.m[y*CW+x],off=S.off[y*CW+x],sq=0;S.sq.forEach(function(q){if(q.i===y*CW+x)sq=q.t/.22});var sc=1,al=1;if(fl){var pr=1-S.clear.t/.38;sc=1+Math.sin(pr*9)*0.05-pr*0.25;al=1-pr*0.6}
  var px=x*C,py=(y-off)*C;c.save();c.translate(px+C/2,py+C/2+sq*3);c.scale(sc*(1+sq*0.06),sc*(1-sq*0.08));tile(c,-C/2,-C/2,v,C,al);c.restore()}
 if(S.piece&&!S.over){var p=S.piece,gy=ghostRow();if(gy>p.y){for(var i=0;i<3;i++){c.save();c.globalAlpha=.22;c.strokeStyle=BASE[p.k[i]][0];c.lineWidth=2;rr(c,p.c*C+4,(gy+i)*C+4,C-8,C-8,7);c.stroke();c.restore()}}
  var fr=canDown()?Math.min(1,S.fallT/interval()):0;for(i=0;i<3;i++)tile(c,p.c*C,(p.y+i+fr)*C,p.k[i],C)}
 c.fillStyle="rgba(10,16,38,.92)";c.fillRect(CW*C,0,PW,H);c.strokeStyle="#536391";c.lineWidth=2;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(CW*C,0);c.lineTo(CW*C,H);c.stroke();
 c.fillStyle="#b3c2dd";c.font="800 13px Quicksand,system-ui,sans-serif";c.textAlign="center";c.fillText("NEXT",CW*C+PW/2,24);for(i=0;i<3;i++)tile(c,CW*C+(PW-C)/2,36+i*C,S.next[i],C);
 c.fillStyle="#74d7ff";c.fillText("Level "+S.level,CW*C+PW/2,190);c.fillStyle="#eef4ff";c.fillText(S.score+" pts",CW*C+PW/2,212);
 c.font="700 10px Quicksand,system-ui,sans-serif";c.fillStyle="#b3c2dd";c.fillText("Clear 3+ of",CW*C+PW/2,250);c.fillText("one kind",CW*C+PW/2,264);
 fx.draw(c);c.restore()}
window.__agcols={start:start,state:function(){return S},step:function(n,dt){for(var i=0;i<n;i++)step(dt||.05)},move:move,rot:rot};

function colBlocks(g,w,h,x){var a=[];for(var y=0;y<h;y++)if(g[y*w+x]===1||g[y*w+x]===4)a.push(y);return a}
function act(a){if(done||dead||!S)return;var bx0=S.x+(a==="S"?S.d:0),by0=S.y+(a==="U"?-1:a==="D"?1:0),wasCrack=a!=="L"&&a!=="R"&&bx0>=0&&bx0<S.w&&by0>=0&&by0<S.h&&S.g[by0*S.w+bx0]===4;

  var ob=colBlocks(S.g,S.w,S.h,bx),nb=colBlocks(n.g,S.w,S.h,bx),sm=a==="D"?S.y+1:a==="U"?S.y-1:S.y;
  var rem=ob.filter(function(y){return y!==sm});for(var i=0;i<nb.length;i++){var d=nb[i]-rem[i];if(d>0)offs[nb[i]*S.w+bx]=d}}
 else PA.beep(420,.03);
 var oy=S.y,ox=S.x,ocr=[];for(var ci=0;ci<S.g.length;ci++)if(S.g[ci]===4)ocr.push(ci);
 S=n;moves++;

function loop(t){raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);if(!document.getElementById("agk")){stop();return}var dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(0.05,(t-last)/1000));last=t;if(!S)return;t0+=dt;fx.update(dt);swing=Math.max(0,swing-dt);

function brick(c,x,y){var pal=eraPal||DEFPAL;var g=c.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+C);g.addColorStop(0,pal[0]);g.addColorStop(1,pal[1]);c.fillStyle=g;rr(c,x+1.5,y+1.5,C-3,C-3,6);c.fill();c.save();rr(c,x+1.5,y+1.5,C-3,C-3,6);c.clip();c.fillStyle="rgba(255,236,210,.85)";for(var r=0;r<4;r++){c.fillRect(x,y+r*10+9,C,1.6);var off=(r%2)*10;for(var q=0;q<3;q++)c.fillRect(x+off+q*20+1,y+r*10,1.6,10)}c.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.2)";c.fillRect(x,y,C,5);c.fillStyle="rgba(60,20,5,.18)";c.fillRect(x,y+C-5,C,5);c.restore();c.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.5)";c.lineWidth=1.3;rr(c,x+2.2,y+2.2,C-4.4,C-4.4,5.5);c.stroke()}

function draw(){var c=ctx,w=S.w,h=S.h,o=fx.off();c.save();c.clearRect(0,0,w*C,h*C);c.translate(o[0],o[1]);c.drawImage(sky,0,0);

 door(c,S.ex*C,S.ey*C);
